VIN_Change_Ho_Support = {
	VIN = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = VIN_Ho_Support
			value = struggle_change
			tooltip = VIN_Ho_Support_Adj_tt
		}
		VIN_refresh_power_struggle = yes
	}
}

VIN_Change_Stalinist_Support = {
	VIN = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = VIN_Stalinist_Support
			value = struggle_change
			tooltip = VIN_Stalinist_Support_Adj_tt
		}
		VIN_refresh_power_struggle = yes
	}
}

VIN_Change_SOV_Favor = {
	VIN = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = VIN_SOV_Favor
			value = struggle_change
			tooltip = VIN_SOV_Favor_Adj_tt
		}
		VIN_refresh_power_struggle = yes
	}
}

VIN_refresh_power_struggle = {
	VIN = {
		clamp_variable = {
			var = VIN_Ho_Support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = VIN_Stalinist_Support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = VIN_SOV_Favor
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { VIN_SOV_Favor_Ho = 100 }
		subtract_from_variable = { VIN_SOV_Favor_Ho = VIN_SOV_Favor }
		add_to_variable = { VIN_Refresh_Variable = 1 }
	}
}

VIN_open_power_struggle = {
	VIN = {
		recruit_character = VIN_Tran_Ngoc_Danh
		set_variable = { VIN_Ho_Support = 70 }
		set_variable = { VIN_Stalinist_Support = 5 }
		set_variable = { VIN_SOV_Favor = 40 }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Struggle_Active
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_soviet_recognition_deferred }
			set_temp_variable = { struggle_change = 15 }
			VIN_Change_Stalinist_Support = yes
			set_temp_variable = { struggle_change = 10 }
			VIN_Change_SOV_Favor = yes
		}
		VIN_refresh_power_struggle = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		activate_mission = VIN_Danh_Coup_Countdown
	}
}

VIN_close_power_struggle = {
	VIN = {
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Struggle_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Struggle_Closed
		VIN_refresh_power_struggle = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

VIN_Danh_purge_conspirators = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VIN_STL_Recruit_Giang_Liem_Army_Group }
			VIN_Dang_Kim_Giang = { remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
			VIN_Nguyen_Van_Vinh = { remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
			VIN_Le_Trong_Nghia = { remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		}
		retire_character = VIN_Tran_Ngoc_Danh
		set_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Coup_Failed
		VIN_close_power_struggle = yes
	}
}

VIN_Danh_resolve_coup = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Coup_Launched } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Coup_Launched
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = VIN_Backs_Danh
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_ai = yes
							has_game_rule = { rule = VIN_ai_stalinist_coup option = VIN_STALINIST_COUP_AI_GUARANTEED }
						}
						AND = {
							has_country_flag = SOV_Permit_Danh_Coup
							check_variable = { VIN_Stalinist_Support > VIN_Ho_Support }
							OR = {
								has_country_flag = SOV_Aid_Danh
								has_country_flag = VIN_soviet_recognition_deferred
								has_country_flag = VIN_Ho_Purged_Left
							}
						}
					}
				}
				country_event = VIN_Danh.20
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = VIN_Backs_Ho
					NOT = {
						AND = {
							is_ai = yes
							has_game_rule = { rule = VIN_ai_stalinist_coup option = VIN_STALINIST_COUP_AI_NEVER }
						}
					}
					check_variable = { VIN_Stalinist_Support > VIN_Ho_Support }
					check_variable = { VIN_SOV_Favor > 84 }
				}
				country_event = VIN_Danh.20
			}
			else = {
				country_event = VIN_Danh.21
			}
		}
	}
}

VIN_party_role_truong_chinh = {
	VIN_Truong_Chinh = {
		if = {
			limit = { VIN = { has_government = trotskyism } }
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc } promote_character = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc }
			else = { add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Truong_Chinh" } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { VIN = { has_government = maoism } }
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = maoist } promote_character = maoist }
			else = { add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = maoist desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Truong_Chinh" } } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { VIN = { has_government = Social_Democratic } }
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_socdem } promote_character = ho_chi_minh_thought_socdem }
			else = { add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_socdem desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Truong_Chinh" } } }
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { has_ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought } promote_character = ho_chi_minh_thought }
			else = { add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Truong_Chinh" } } }
		}
	}
}

VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh }
		VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought }
				remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc }
				remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_ideology = maoist }
				remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = maoist }
			}
		}
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_truong_chinh = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Truong_Chinh }
			VIN_party_role_truong_chinh = yes
			VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = yes
		}
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_ho_chi_minh = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh }
			VIN_party_role_ho_chi_minh = yes
		}
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_le_duan = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
				country_exists = NLF
				NLF = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
			}
			NLF = { nlf_return_le_duan_north = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
			VIN_party_role_le_duan = yes
			VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = yes
		}
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_vo_nguyen_giap = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Vo_Nguyen_Giap }
			VIN_party_role_vo_nguyen_giap = yes
			VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = yes
		}
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_le_duc_tho = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = NLF
				NLF = { has_character = NLF_Le_Duc_Tho }
			}
			NLF = { retire_character = NLF_Le_Duc_Tho }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duc_Tho } }
			recruit_character = VIN_Le_Duc_Tho
		}
		VIN_party_role_le_duc_tho = yes
		VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = yes
	}
}

VIN_set_general_secretary_tran_ngoc_danh = {
	VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Tran_Ngoc_Danh }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = communism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			VIN_Tran_Ngoc_Danh = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = { ideology = stalinism desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Tran_Ngoc_Danh" }
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.4
			}
			VIN_release_ho_chi_minh_party_role = yes
		}
	}
}

VIN_vacate_presidency = {
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Ho_Chi_Minh
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Ton_Duc_Thang
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Nguyen_Huu_Tho
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Truong_Chinh
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Vo_Chi_Cong
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Le_Duc_Anh
	clr_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Tran_Duc_Luong
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_President_Ho_Chi_Minh }
		remove_ideas = VIN_President_Ho_Chi_Minh
	}
}

VIN_set_president_ho_chi_minh = {
	VIN = {
		hidden_effect = {
			VIN_vacate_presidency = yes
			set_country_flag = CWIC_HOG_UI_ENABLED
			set_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Ho_Chi_Minh
		}
		add_ideas = VIN_President_Ho_Chi_Minh
	}
}

VIN_leadership_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1969.9.2
			has_character = VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh ruling_only = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = Social_Democratic }
				VIN_set_premier_pham_van_dong_as_leader = yes
			}
			else = {
				VIN_set_general_secretary_le_duan = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Ho_Chi_Minh }
			VIN_set_president_ton_duc_thang = yes
		}
		retire_character = VIN_Ho_Chi_Minh
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1967.7.6
			has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Nguyen_Chi_Thanh ruling_only = yes }
		}
		VIN_set_general_secretary_le_duan = yes
		retire_character = VIN_Nguyen_Chi_Thanh
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1980.3.30
			has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Ton_Duc_Thang
		}
		VIN_set_president_nguyen_huu_tho = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1981.7.4
			has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Nguyen_Huu_Tho
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VIN_Truong_Chinh }
			VIN_set_president_truong_chinh = yes
		}
		else = {
			VIN_set_president_vo_chi_cong = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1986.7.10
			has_character = VIN_Le_Duan
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Le_Duan ruling_only = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = VIN_Truong_Chinh }
				VIN_set_general_secretary_truong_chinh = yes
			}
			else = {
				VIN_set_general_secretary_nguyen_van_linh = yes
			}
		}
		retire_character = VIN_Le_Duan
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1986.12.18
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Danh_Coup_Succeeded }
			OR = {
				has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Truong_Chinh ruling_only = yes }
				has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Vo_Nguyen_Giap ruling_only = yes }
				has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Le_Duc_Tho ruling_only = yes }
			}
		}
		VIN_set_general_secretary_nguyen_van_linh = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1987.6.17
			has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Truong_Chinh
		}
		VIN_set_president_vo_chi_cong = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1988.9.30
			has_character = VIN_Truong_Chinh
			NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Truong_Chinh ruling_only = yes } }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Truong_Chinh }
		}
		retire_character = VIN_Truong_Chinh
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1991.6.27
			has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Nguyen_Van_Linh ruling_only = yes }
		}
		VIN_set_general_secretary_do_muoi = yes
		retire_character = VIN_Nguyen_Van_Linh
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1992.9.23
			has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Vo_Chi_Cong
		}
		VIN_set_president_le_duc_anh = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1997.9.24
			has_country_flag = VIN_HoG_Le_Duc_Anh
		}
		VIN_set_president_tran_duc_luong = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1997.12.26
			has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Do_Muoi ruling_only = yes }
		}
		VIN_set_general_secretary_le_kha_phieu = yes
		retire_character = VIN_Do_Muoi
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 2001.4.22
			has_country_leader = { character = VIN_Le_Kha_Phieu ruling_only = yes }
		}
		VIN_set_general_secretary_nong_duc_manh = yes
		retire_character = VIN_Le_Kha_Phieu
	}
}

NLF_Victory = {
	NLF = {
		annex_country = {
			target = VIE
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = FUL
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = CCC
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		set_capital	= { state = 286	}
		set_cosmetic_tag = NLF_VIE
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh
				NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Binh_Killed }
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = communism
				elections_allowed = no
				long_name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT_long
				name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT
			}
			NLF_Nguyen_Binh = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = communism
				long_name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT_long
				name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = trotskyism
				elections_allowed = no
				long_name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT_CN_long
				name = VIE_PARTY_CPCMLT_CN
			}
			set_popularities = {
				democratic = 0
				communism = 50
				fascism = 0
				nationalist = 0
				monarchism = 0
				neutrality = 0
				islamism = 0
				radical_shiaism = 0
				Social_Democratic = 0
				Social_Liberalism = 0
				Salafism = 0
				Liberal_Conservatism = 0
				conservative = 0
				Christian_Democratic = 0
				socialist = 0
				centrist = 0
				liberal = 0
				trotskyism = 50
				maoism = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { NLF_southern_command_settled_trigger = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 5 }
				nlf_set_southern_command = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col = 0 }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col = 0 }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col = 0 }

xiangqi_add_legal_attack = {

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count > 4 } }
		hidden_effect = { }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 1 }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
	}
}

xiangqi_add_legal_move = {
	# Expects:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
			xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes
		}
		xiangqi_add_legal_attack = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
			xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes
		}
		xiangqi_add_legal_attack = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count > 24 } }
		hidden_effect = { }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 1 }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_6_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_6_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_7_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_7_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_8_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_8_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_9_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_9_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_10_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_10_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }

xiangqi_government_elephant_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col }


		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

xiangqi_government_horse_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {

				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }

				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }

			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 }
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

# ============================================================
# Rule:
#   - First occupied square sets "screen_found = 1" and continues.
# ============================================================

xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = {
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 11 } }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Off-board?
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Check square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_attacks = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } }


        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_down = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_left = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_right = yes
    }
}

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves = {
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_down = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_up = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = yes


	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_down = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_left = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_right = yes
}

xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}

xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 }
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_up = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 }
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves = {
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = 0 }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_down = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_up = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }
}

xiangqi_government_soldier_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col > 5 } }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

xiangqi_government_general_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col }

		# Rows (horizontal): 4..6  => row > 3 AND row < 7
		# Cols (vertical):   1..3  => col > 0 AND col < 4

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}
	}
}

Potential_Move_1_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }

	else_if = {

			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	else_if = {

			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup

	}
	else_if = {

			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

	}

		}

xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = {
	# Expects:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_move_index (1..24)
	# Uses:

	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 1 } }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 17 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 18 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 19 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 22 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 24 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_col } }
	else = {
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 1 }

	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 1 }
	}


	# Government (side = 1)
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
	}

	#Reformist
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
	}


	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 0 }
	set_variable = {xiangqi_selected_row = 0 }
	set_variable = {xiangqi_selected_col = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
}

#Reformist Moves
xiangqi_reformist_elephant_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col }


		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

xiangqi_reformist_horse_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 }
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_up = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

# ============================================================
# Rule:
#   - First occupied square sets "screen_found = 1" and continues.
# ============================================================

xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_up = {
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 11 } }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Off-board?
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Check square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_down = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
