}

vin_campaign_open_planned_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive }
	}
}

vin_campaign_apply_planned_offensive = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } } }
		1761 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } }
		1761 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
		1766 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
		1761 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
		671 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
		1280 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
		1768 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } }
		1761 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 } }
		786 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } }
		786 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } }
		671 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } }
		1761 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } }
		671 = { vin_campaign_open_planned_state = yes }
	}
}

vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_controlled_by = VIN
			VIN = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network
				days = -1
			}
		}
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_campaign_rear_supply_network }
		}
	}
}

vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply = {
	1762 = { vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply_state = yes }
	1763 = { vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply_state = yes }
	838 = { vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply_state = yes }
}

vin_campaign_sync_objective_modifiers = {
	vin_campaign_restore_northern_front = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } }
		vin_campaign_apply_planned_offensive = yes
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } } }
			1761 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } }
			1761 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
			1766 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
			1761 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
			671 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
			1280 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
			1768 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } } 1761 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 } } 786 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } } 786 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } } 671 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } } 1761 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } } 671 = { vin_apply_contested_objective = yes } }
	}
	vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply = yes
	671 = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege }
				NOT = { indochina_french_held_state_trigger = yes }
			}
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege }
		}
	}
}

vin_apply_contested_objective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			indochina_french_held_state_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective }
	}
}

vin_campaign_update_french_overextension = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI } TAI = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM } TAM = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN } NUN = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO } THO = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { if = { limit = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } add_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } else_if = { limit = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension NOT = { indochina_french_aligned_controls_vin_rear_trigger = yes } } remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } } }
}

vin_campaign_clear_french_overextension = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		FRE = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		TAI = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		TAM = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NUN  NUN = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		NUN = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		THO = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE  VIE = { has_idea = FRE_Campaign_Overextension } }
		VIE = { remove_ideas = FRE_Campaign_Overextension }
	}
}

vin_overextension_severe_apply = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe } }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension } remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension }
		add_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe
		log = "IC_AFK|OVEREXTENSION|date=[GetDateText]|tier=severe|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|state=apply"
		ic_afk_validation_vin_severe_cause = yes
		vin_laos_raid_modifier_sync = yes
	}
}

vin_overextension_severe_clear = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe }
		remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe
		log = "IC_AFK|OVEREXTENSION|date=[GetDateText]|tier=severe|campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]|state=clear"
		vin_laos_raid_modifier_sync = yes
	}
}

vin_campaign_update_overextension = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				vin_campaign_severe_overextension_trigger = yes
				vin_all_in_corridor_overextended_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		vin_overextension_severe_apply = yes
	}
	else = {
		vin_overextension_severe_clear = yes
	}
	# Ordinary tier: only off-envelope ground held 5 days in a row counts, so a
	# border grab in the first days of a declaration does not penalise the
	# whole army. The severe tier above stays immediate.
	if = {
		limit = {
			vin_campaign_vin_overextended_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe }
		}
		add_to_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension }
				check_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days > 4 }
			}
			add_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension }
			remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension
		}
	}
	vin_campaign_update_french_overextension = yes
}

vin_campaign_apply_war_spirits = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Mobilization } }
		add_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Mobilization
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } }
		FRE = { add_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } }
		TAI = { add_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } }
		TAM = { add_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NUN  NUN = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } }
		NUN = { add_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } } }
		THO = { add_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support } remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support }
}

vin_campaign_remove_war_spirits = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Mobilization }
		remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Mobilization
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } }
		FRE = { remove_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } }
		TAI = { remove_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } }
		TAM = { remove_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NUN  NUN = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } }
		NUN = { remove_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO  THO = { has_idea = FRE_Highland_Overextension } }
		THO = { remove_ideas = FRE_Highland_Overextension }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support }
		remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension }
		remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_Overextension
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { vin_all_in_corridor_overextended_trigger = yes } }
		vin_overextension_severe_clear = yes
	}
	vin_campaign_clear_french_overextension = yes
}

vin_northwest_recovery_clear_war_snapshot = {
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRE_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRA_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAI_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAM_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_THO_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_VIE_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_NUN_War
}

vin_northwest_recovery_snapshot_preexisting_wars = {
	vin_northwest_recovery_clear_war_snapshot = yes
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Cleanup_Complete
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE has_war_with = FRE } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRE_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA has_war_with = FRA } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRA_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAI_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM has_war_with = TAM } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAM_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO has_war_with = THO } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_THO_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_VIE_War }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN has_war_with = NUN } set_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_NUN_War }
	}
	else = { log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY inherited_campaign_armistice_not_preserved" }
	log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY preexisting_wars_snapshotted"
}

vin_campaign_declare_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending }
		vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars = yes
	}
	vin_campaign_open_corridors = yes
	vin_campaign_restore_unplanned_offensive = yes
	vin_campaign_sync_objective_modifiers = yes
	vin_campaign_apply_war_spirits = yes
	set_variable = { VIN_Overextension_Streak_Days = 0 }
	vin_campaign_update_overextension = yes
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed = 0 }
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 120 }	# a clean campaign
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 210 }	# past this the offensive has failed
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 150 }	# the valley is a siege, not a march
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 260 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 20 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 35 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 20 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 35 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 30 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 50 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 30 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 45 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 25 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 40 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = 30 }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 45 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_x = -1 }	# every recovery success is costly
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_deadline_y = 120 }
	}
	vin_campaign_apply_command_inputs = yes
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_timer_days = VIN_camp_deadline_y }
	add_to_variable = { VIN_camp_timer_days = 1 }
	vin_limited_campaign_add_objective_vps = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 }
			country_exists = THO
			THO = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1280 size > 0 } }
					NOT = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1768 size > 0 } }
				}
			}
		}
		THO = {
			delete_units = {
				division_template = "Auxiliaires territoriaux tho"
				disband = yes
			}
		}
		1280 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"1er Bataillon territorial de Cao Bang\" division_template = \"Auxiliaires territoriaux tho\" start_experience_factor = 0.45 start_equipment_factor = 0.9"
				owner = THO
				prioritize_location = 12065
			}
		}
		1768 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"2e Bataillon territorial de Lang Son\" division_template = \"Auxiliaires territoriaux tho\" start_experience_factor = 0.45 start_equipment_factor = 0.9"
				owner = THO
				prioritize_location = 9948
			}
		}
		log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: THO historical garrisons re-formed for Cao-Bac declaration"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 }
			country_exists = TAM
			NOT = { has_war_with = TAM }
		}
		hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = TAM type = annex_everything } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 }
			country_exists = THO
			NOT = { has_war_with = THO }
		}
		hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = THO type = annex_everything } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 }
			}
			country_exists = TAI
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Owns_TAI_War
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = TAI } }
			hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = TAI type = annex_everything } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } VIN_northwest_recovery_target_available_trigger = yes }
		vin_northwest_recovery_snapshot_preexisting_wars = yes
		1761 = { owner = { save_global_event_target_as = VIN_northwest_recovery_target } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:VIN_northwest_recovery_target } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War
			hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = event_target:VIN_northwest_recovery_target type = annex_everything } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 }
			}
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Owns_FRE_War
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = FRE } }
			hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = FRE type = annex_everything } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 }
			}
			country_exists = TAI
			NOT = { has_war_with = TAI }
		}
		hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = TAI type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } } }
		indochina_call_all_vin_fre_theater_forces = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
		lao_join_the_battle_of_dien_bien_phu = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = {
				fre_castor_handoff_to_campaign = yes
				fre_pollux_handoff_to_campaign = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Hedgehog_Network_Chosen } }
					fre_dbp_response_begin = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 } } activate_mission = VIN_Vinh_Yen_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 } } activate_mission = VIN_Mao_Khe_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } } activate_mission = VIN_Day_River_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 } } activate_mission = VIN_Na_San_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 } } activate_mission = VIN_Nghia_Lo_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 } } activate_mission = VIN_Lai_Chau_Objective }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } } activate_mission = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Objective }
	else = { activate_mission = VIN_Campaign_Objective_Timer }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 }
			country_exists = FRE
			1766 = { indochina_french_held_state_trigger = yes }
		}
		FRE = { fre_hoa_binh_response_begin = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current > 4 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current < 9 } }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 }
			}
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_preparation_response_begin = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 }
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_pollux_lai_chau_offer = yes }
	}
	ic_afk_validation_vin_launch_tick = yes
}

vin_limited_campaign_add_objective_vps = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 5 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Vinh_Yen }
		}
		1761 = { add_victory_points = { province = 12075 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Vinh_Yen
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 6 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Mao_Khe }
		}
		786 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13772 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Mao_Khe
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Day_River }
		}
		786 = {
			add_victory_points = { province = 1185 value = 1 }
			add_victory_points = { province = 13753 value = 1 }
			add_victory_points = { province = 13755 value = 1 }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Day_River
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 8 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Na_San }
		}
		671 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13757 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Na_San
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Nghia_Lo }
		}
		1761 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13773 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Nghia_Lo
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Lai_Chau }
		}
		671 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13765 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Lai_Chau
	}
}

		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Vinh_Yen
	}
	if = {

		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Mao_Khe
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Day_River }
		786 = {
			add_victory_points = { province = 1185 value = -1 }
			add_victory_points = { province = 13753 value = -1 }
			add_victory_points = { province = 13755 value = -1 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Day_River
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Na_San }
		671 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13757 value = -1 } }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Na_San
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Nghia_Lo }
		1761 = { add_victory_points = { province = 13773 value = -1 } }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Nghia_Lo

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Lai_Chau }

		clr_country_flag = VIN_Temporary_Objective_VP_Lai_Chau
	}
}

vin_campaign_objective_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending }
		vin_northwest_recovery_close_owned_war = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending }
		vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { VIN_no_campaign_running_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving }
		}
		vin_limited_campaign_add_objective_vps = yes
		add_to_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed = 1 }
		vin_limited_campaign_update_objective_hold = yes
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support } remove_ideas = VIN_Campaign_AI_Support }
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_Indochina_AI_Support } remove_ideas = VIN_Indochina_AI_Support }
		vin_campaign_sync_objective_modifiers = yes
		vin_campaign_update_overextension = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = {
				fre_lorraine_response_daily_tick = yes
				fre_hoa_binh_response_daily_tick = yes
				fre_dbp_response_daily_tick = yes
			}
		}
		vin_campaign_check_resolution = yes
	}
}

vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Limited_Operation_Active }
		}
		log = "FRE_OP_TRACE: VIN_ARMISTICE yield_to_active_FRE_operation vin_campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0]"
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE  has_war_with = FRE }
			log = "FRE_OP_TRACE: WHITE_PEACE caller=vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars actor=VIN target=FRE vin_campaign=[?VIN_camp_current|0] vin_day=[?VIN_camp_days_elapsed|0]"
			white_peace = FRE
		}
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA  has_war_with = FRA } white_peace = FRA }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI  has_war_with = TAI } white_peace = TAI }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM  has_war_with = TAM } white_peace = TAM }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = THO  has_war_with = THO } white_peace = THO }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE  has_war_with = VIE } white_peace = VIE }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN  has_war_with = NUN } white_peace = NUN }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						AND = { country_exists = FRE  has_war_with = FRE }
						AND = { country_exists = FRA  has_war_with = FRA }
						AND = { country_exists = TAI  has_war_with = TAI }
						AND = { country_exists = TAM  has_war_with = TAM }
						AND = { country_exists = THO  has_war_with = THO }
						AND = { country_exists = VIE  has_war_with = VIE }
						AND = { country_exists = NUN  has_war_with = NUN }
					}
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending
		}
	}
}

vin_limited_campaign_update_objective_hold = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current > 4 }
			vin_limited_campaign_raw_objective_trigger = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current > 4 } }
		set_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 0 }
	}
}

vin_campaign_check_resolution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			vin_campaign_objective_met_trigger = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed > VIN_camp_deadline_y } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed > VIN_camp_deadline_x } } }
			set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 1 }	# clean success, inclusive boundary
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 2 }	# costly success
		}
		vin_campaign_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { vin_campaign_superseded_trigger = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 5 }
		vin_campaign_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 }
			has_country_flag = VIN_Hoa_Binh_Amarante_Withdrawal_Ready
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Hoa_Binh_Amarante_Withdrawal_Ready
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 1 }	# the parent owns Amarante's voluntary withdrawal result
		vin_campaign_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed > VIN_camp_deadline_y } }
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 4 }
		vin_campaign_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { vin_campaign_war_abandoned_trigger = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 3 }
		vin_campaign_resolve = yes
	}
}

vin_campaign_backstop_resolve = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			vin_campaign_objective_met_trigger = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed > VIN_camp_deadline_y } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_days_elapsed > VIN_camp_deadline_x } } }
			set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 2 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { vin_campaign_superseded_trigger = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 }
			has_country_flag = VIN_Hoa_Binh_Amarante_Withdrawal_Ready
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Hoa_Binh_Amarante_Withdrawal_Ready
		set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 1 }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { vin_camp_outcome = 4 } }
	vin_campaign_resolve = yes
}

vin_campaign_record_result = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Result_Clean

vin_campaign_resolve = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving	# the tick must not re-enter while the outcome is being paid out
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = vin_camp_outcome }
	vin_campaign_record_result = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 }
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_lorraine_resolve_from_northwest = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 }
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_hoa_binh_resolve_from_campaign = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 }
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_dbp_resolve_from_campaign = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current > 4 } check_variable = { VIN_camp_current < 9 } }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 10 }
				check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 11 }
			}
			country_exists = FRE
		}
		FRE = { fre_preparation_resolve_from_campaign = yes }
	}
	vin_campaign_remove_war_spirits = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 5 } }
			NOT = { fre_hedgehog_network_chosen_trigger = yes }
		}
		vin_dbp_record_outcome = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 3 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 1 } }
			transfer_state = 1761
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { NOT = { owns_state = 671 } } }
				annex_country = { target = TAI transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } }
			transfer_state = 1766
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = TAM  TAM = { num_of_controlled_states < 1 } }
				annex_country = { target = TAM transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
			transfer_state = 671
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = TAI }
				annex_country = { target = TAI transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
			transfer_state = 1280
			transfer_state = 1768
			set_country_flag = Enable_Air_Base_Raids
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = THO }
				annex_country = { target = THO transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } }
			transfer_state = 1761
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = TAI  TAI = { NOT = { owns_state = 671 } num_of_controlled_states < 1 } }
				annex_country = { target = TAI transfer_troops = no }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { indochina_phase_amount = 100 }
		indochina_struggle_escalate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 11 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 25 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = -10 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 25
				tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -10
				tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								has_country_flag = FRE_Hoa_Binh_Result_Withdrawal
								VIN = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 2 } }
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = -12 }
					fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 2 } }
		set_temp_variable = { indochina_phase_amount = 50 }
		indochina_struggle_escalate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 11 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 10 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = -5 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 10
				tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 5
				tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = {
				set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = -4 }
				fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 60 }
				fre_change_war_credits = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 3 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 11 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = -10 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = 10 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = -10
				tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 10
				tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome = 4 } }
		set_temp_variable = { indochina_phase_amount = 50 }
		indochina_struggle_deescalate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 11 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = -20 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = 20 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = -20
				tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 20
				tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = {
				set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = 10 }
				fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 120 }
				fre_change_war_credits = yes
			}
		}
	}
	FRA = { indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes }
	hidden_effect = { vin_notice_campaign_result = yes }
	vin_campaign_finish = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 3 } }
		country_event = { id = VIN_Camp.4 hours = 4 }	# the valley reads differently to a march
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = VIN_Camp.2 hours = 4 }	# the campaign never reported its own result
	}
}

vin_northwest_recovery_close_owned_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War
			event_target:VIN_northwest_recovery_target = { exists = yes }
			has_war_with = event_target:VIN_northwest_recovery_target
		}
		log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=dynamic"
		white_peace = event_target:VIN_northwest_recovery_target
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE has_war_with = FRE NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRE_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=FRE" white_peace = FRE }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA has_war_with = FRA NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRA_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=FRA" white_peace = FRA }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAI_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=TAI" white_peace = TAI }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TAM has_war_with = TAM NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAM_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=TAM" white_peace = TAM }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = THO has_war_with = THO NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_THO_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=THO" white_peace = THO }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_VIE_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=VIE" white_peace = VIE }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NUN has_war_with = NUN NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_NUN_War } } log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY WHITE_PEACE target=NUN" white_peace = NUN }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { country_exists = FRE has_war_with = FRE NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRE_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = FRA has_war_with = FRA NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_FRA_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = TAI has_war_with = TAI NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAI_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = TAM has_war_with = TAM NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_TAM_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = THO has_war_with = THO NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_THO_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = VIE has_war_with = VIE NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_VIE_War } }
					AND = { country_exists = NUN has_war_with = NUN NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Preexisting_NUN_War } }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "VIN_CAMPAIGN_TRACE: NW_RECOVERY cleanup_complete"
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Owns_War
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Cleanup_Complete
		clear_global_event_target = VIN_northwest_recovery_target
		vin_northwest_recovery_clear_war_snapshot = yes
	}
}

vin_campaign_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Active
						has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Deep
						has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Limited
						has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Withheld
						has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Objective_Achieved
						has_country_flag = FRE_Mission_Operation_Lorraine_Activated
						has_idea = FRE_Lorraine_Deep_Columns
						has_idea = FRE_Northwest_Concentrated_Defence
					}
				}
				fre_lorraine_response_cleanup = yes
			}
			fre_hoa_binh_response_cleanup = yes
			fre_dbp_response_cleanup = yes
			fre_preparation_response_cleanup = yes
		}
	}
	fre_preparation_disband_garrison = yes
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Hoa_Binh_Amarante_Withdrawal_Ready
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Cao_Bac
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Tay_Bac
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Hoa_Binh
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Dien_Bien
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Vinh_Yen_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Vinh_Yen_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Vinh_Yen
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Mao_Khe_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Mao_Khe_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Mao_Khe
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Day_River_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Day_River_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Day_River
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Na_San_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Na_San_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Na_San
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Nghia_Lo_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Nghia_Lo_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Nghia_Lo
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Lai_Chau_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Lai_Chau_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Lai_Chau
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Active
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Cooldown_Northwest_Recovery
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Northwest_Recovery_Armistice_Pending
		vin_northwest_recovery_close_owned_war = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Armistice_Pending
		vin_limited_campaign_close_spillover_wars = yes
	}
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Owns_TAI_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Command_Inputs_Applied
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Limited_Campaign_Owns_FRE_War
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = THO }
		vin_restore_tho_to_french_command = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TAI }
		TAI = { fre_tai_highlands_relief_disband = yes }
	}
	fre_dbp_disband_gono = yes
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 0 }
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = indochina_contested_objective }
	}
	vin_campaign_restore_unplanned_offensive = yes
	vin_campaign_sync_rear_supply = yes
	vin_campaign_remove_war_spirits = yes
	vin_limited_campaign_remove_objective_vps = yes
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_objective_hold_days = 0 }
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving
	FRA = { remove_mission = VIN_Campaign_Objective_Timer }	# compatibility: old saves hosted this timer on France
	remove_mission = VIN_Campaign_Objective_Timer
	remove_mission = VIN_Vinh_Yen_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Mao_Khe_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Day_River_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Na_San_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Nghia_Lo_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Lai_Chau_Objective
	remove_mission = VIN_Northwest_Recovery_Objective
	set_variable = { VIN_camp_timer_days = 0 }
}

vin_dbp_record_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_dbp_outcome_applied }
			FRE = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Result_Victory }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Result_Fall }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Result_Breakout }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { controls_province = 4529 }
			FRE = { set_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Result_Fall }
			news_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.30 hours = 18 }
		}
		else = {
			FRE = { set_country_flag = FRE_DBP_Result_Victory }
			news_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.31 hours = 18 }
		}
	}
	671 = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege }
		}
	}
}

fre_dbp_fortify_camp = {
	if = {
		limit = { fre_hedgehog_network_chosen_trigger = yes }
		set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 0 }
		log = "IC_AFK|HEDGEHOG|date=[GetDateText]|stage=dbp_camp_not_built"
	}
	else = {
		fre_dbp_fortify_valley_camp = yes
	}
}

fre_dbp_fortify_valley_camp = {
	671 = {
		set_victory_points = {
			province = 13765
			value = 1
		}
		set_victory_points = {
			province = 4529
			value = 10
		}
		set_building_level = { type = bunker level = 4 province = { id = 4529 } }
		set_building_level = { type = anti_air_building level = 2 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				VIN = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active }
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege } }
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = dbp_camp_under_siege }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 4 }
	fre_dbp_deploy_gono = yes
}

fre_dbp_deploy_gono = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed }
		log = "[GetDateText]: fre_dbp_deploy_gono re-entered and refused - the valley already has its garrison"
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { gono_tier = 1 }	# floor: 3 groupements at every tier; +1 at tier 1, +2 and stocks at tier 2
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_Metropole_Patience > 65 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { gono_tier = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { country_exists = FRE }
					FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_Metropole_Patience < 33 } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { gono_tier = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Full_Drop } }
			set_temp_variable = { gono_tier = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Castor_Limited_Drop } }
			set_temp_variable = { gono_tier = 0 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE  FRE = { has_subject = TAI } }
			set_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed
			set_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Is_FRE
			FRE = { fre_dbp_gono_template_cefeo = yes }
			671 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - 1er Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.6 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = FRE
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - 2e Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.55 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = FRE
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - Groupement Aeroporte\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.65 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = FRE
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { gono_tier > 0 } }
				671 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"GONO - Bataillon de Marche Etranger\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.6 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
						owner = FRE
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
						prioritize_location = 4529
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { gono_tier > 1 } }
				671 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"GONO - 3e Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.55 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
						owner = FRE
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
						prioritize_location = 4529
					}
				}
				FRE = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 1500 producer = FRA }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 200 producer = FRA }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|DBP_GARRISON|date=[GetDateText]|owner=FRE|tier=[?gono_tier|0]|patience=[?FRE.FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]"
			news_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.32 hours = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { country_exists = TAI }
			set_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed
			TAI = { fre_dbp_gono_template_tai = yes }
			671 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - 1er Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.6 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = TAI
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - 2e Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.55 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = TAI
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"GONO - Groupement Aeroporte\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.65 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
					owner = TAI
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
					prioritize_location = 4529
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { gono_tier > 0 } }
				671 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"GONO - Bataillon de Marche Etranger\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.6 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
						owner = TAI
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
						prioritize_location = 4529
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { gono_tier > 1 } }
				671 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"GONO - 3e Groupement\" division_template = \"Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest\" start_experience_factor = 0.55 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
						owner = TAI
						allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
						prioritize_location = 4529
					}
				}
				TAI = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 1500 producer = FRA }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 200 producer = FRA }
				}
			}
			log = "IC_AFK|DBP_GARRISON|date=[GetDateText]|owner=TAI|tier=[?gono_tier|0]|patience=[?FRE.FRE_Metropole_Patience|0]"
			news_event = { id = SWF_Indochina_War.32 hours = 3 }
		}
	}
}

fre_dbp_gono_template_cefeo = {
	division_template = {
		name = "Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest"
		regiments = {
			airborne = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			airborne = { x = 0 y = 1 }
			infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			artillery = { x = 2 y = 0 }
		}
		support = {
			engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			field_hospital = { x = 0 y = 1 }
		}
		priority = 0
	}
}

fre_dbp_gono_template_tai = {
	division_template = {
		name = "Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest"
		regiments = {
			infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
			infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			militia = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			artillery = { x = 2 y = 0 }
		}
		support = {
			engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			recon = { x = 0 y = 1 }
		}
		priority = 0
	}
}

fre_dbp_disband_gono = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed }
		clr_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Deployed
		clr_global_flag = VIN_DBP_Garrison_Is_FRE
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = { delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest" } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = TAI }
			TAI = { delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Groupement Operationnel du Nord-Ouest" } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = { clr_country_flag = FRE_Pollux_Survivor_Group_Deployed }
		}
	}
}


vin_dbp_siege_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving }
			NOT = { controls_province = 4529 }
			vin_dbp_valley_invested_trigger = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days = 1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days > 13 }
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level > 3 }
			}
			set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 3 }
			671 = { set_building_level = { type = bunker level = 3 province = { id = 4529 } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days > 27 }
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level > 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 2 }
			671 = { set_building_level = { type = bunker level = 2 province = { id = 4529 } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days > 41 }
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level > 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 1 }
			671 = { set_building_level = { type = bunker level = 1 province = { id = 4529 } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days > 55 }
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level = 0 }
			671 = {
				set_building_level = { type = bunker level = 0 province = { id = 4529 } }
				set_building_level = { type = anti_air_building level = 0 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_siege_days > 55 }
				check_variable = { global.VIN_dbp_fort_level < 1 }
			}
			fre_dbp_disband_gono = yes
			set_province_controller = 4529
			log = "[GetDateText]: Dien Bien Phu overrun by an AI siege after [?global.VIN_dbp_siege_days|0] days"
		}
	}
}


vin_sync_guerilla_networks = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			is_core_of = VIN
			NOT = { is_core_of = MEO }
			NOT = { is_owned_by = VIE }
			NOT = { is_owned_by = CCC }
			NOT = { is_owned_by = FUL }
			NOT = { is_owned_by = NLF }
			NOT = { is_controlled_by = VIN }
			NOT = { vin_aligned_state_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_guerilla_network } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_guerilla_network }
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_guerilla_network }
			OR = {
				is_controlled_by = VIN
				is_owned_by = VIE
				is_owned_by = CCC
				is_owned_by = FUL
				is_owned_by = NLF
				vin_aligned_state_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VIN_guerilla_network }
	}
}


vin_campaign_watchdog = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Resolving }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = { flag = VIN_Campaign_Cao_Bac_Active days > 150 }
				has_country_flag = { flag = VIN_Campaign_Tay_Bac_Active days > 150 }
				has_country_flag = { flag = VIN_Campaign_Hoa_Binh_Active days > 150 }
				has_country_flag = { flag = VIN_Campaign_Dien_Bien_Active days > 150 }
			}
		}
		vin_campaign_check_resolution = yes
	}
}

vin_notice_campaign_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Intel.1 days = 1 } }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=FRE_Intel.1|to=FRE"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = THO }
			THO = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_VIN.1 days = 1 } }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.1|to=THO"
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 9 } }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Intel.3 days = 1 } }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=FRE_Intel.3|to=FRE"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = TAI }
			TAI = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_VIN.11 days = 1 } }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.11|to=TAI"
		}
	}
}

vin_notice_campaign_result = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 4 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 5 } }
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = FRE }
				FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Intel.2 days = 1 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=FRE_Intel.2|to=FRE"
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 5 } }
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = NUN }
				NUN = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_VIN.2 days = 1 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.2|to=NUN"
			}
		}

			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 5 } }

				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.3|to=FRA"
			}
		}

			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 3 } }

				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.4|to=PRC"
			}

			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 3 } }

				VIE = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_VIN.6 days = 1 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.6|to=VIE"

		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_current = 7 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { VIN_camp_last_outcome < 5 } }
			if = {

				FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Intel.7 days = 1 } }

vin_notice_lower_laos_result = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained } }
		FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Intel.11 days = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=FRE_Intel.11|to=FRE"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained } }
		VIN = { country_event = { id = VIN_Notice.1 days = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_Notice.1|to=VIN"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = LOS OR = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained } }
		LOS = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_VIN.18 days = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_VIN.18|to=LOS"
	}
}

# Observer-only CEFEO operation intelligence for a VIN player. These helpers
# never resolve an operation, alter its result, declare peace or award score.

vin_cefeo_intel_launch_lorraine = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = {
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Concluded
		set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Active
		if = { limit = { OR = { FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Deep } FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Lorraine_Response_Limited } } } activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Lorraine }
		country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.1 hours = 1 }
		log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Lorraine"
	} }
}

vin_cefeo_intel_finish_lorraine = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = {
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Active } set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Concluded }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Active
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Lorraine
		log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CLEANUP|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Lorraine"
	} }
}

vin_cefeo_intel_launch_limited_operation = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 2 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Hirondelle
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.2 hours = 1 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Hirondelle"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 3 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Mouette
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.4 hours = 1 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Mouette"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 4 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Brochet
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.6 hours = 1 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Brochet"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 5 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Bretagne
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.14 hours = 1 }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.14|to=VIN"
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Bretagne"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 6 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Adolphe
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.16 hours = 1 }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.16|to=VIN"
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Adolphe"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 7 } } }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Concluded
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Active
			activate_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Camargue
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.18 hours = 1 }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.18|to=VIN"
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|LAUNCH|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Camargue"
		}
	} }
}

vin_cefeo_intel_finish_limited_operation = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 2 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Hirondelle
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.3 hours = 4 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Hirondelle"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 3 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Mouette
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.5 hours = 4 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Mouette"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 4 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Brochet
			country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.7 hours = 4 }
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Brochet"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 5 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Bretagne
			if = {
				limit = { FRE = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Bretagne_Result_Superseded } } }
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.15 hours = 4 }
					log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.15|to=VIN"
				}
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Bretagne"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 6 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Adolphe
			if = {
				limit = { FRE = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Adolphe_Result_Superseded } } }
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.17 hours = 4 }
					log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.17|to=VIN"
				}
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Adolphe"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { FRE = { check_variable = { FRE_op_current = 7 } } has_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Active }
			clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Active
			set_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Concluded
			remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Camargue
			if = {
				limit = { FRE = { NOT = { has_country_flag = FRE_Operation_Camargue_Result_Superseded } } }
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.19 hours = 4 }
					log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.19|to=VIN"
				}
			}
			log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CONCLUSION|date=[GetDateText]|operation=Camargue"
		}
	} }
}

vin_cefeo_intel_clear_all = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIN } VIN = {
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Lorraine_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Hirondelle_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Mouette_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Brochet_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Bretagne_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Adolphe_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Camargue_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Castor_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Pollux_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_CEFEO_Intel_Atlante_Active
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Lorraine
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Hirondelle
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Mouette
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Brochet
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Bretagne
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Adolphe
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Camargue
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Castor
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Pollux
		remove_mission = VIN_Intel_Operation_Atlante
		log = "CEFEO_INTEL|CLEANUP|date=[GetDateText]|operation=all"
	} }
}

nlf_dak_doa_begin = {
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Clean
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Costly
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Aborted
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Failure
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded
	set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active
	set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed = 0 }
	set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 0 }
	set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP } }
		1286 = { add_victory_points = { province = 1328 value = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FUL NOT = { has_war_with = FUL } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Owns_FUL_War
		hidden_effect = { declare_war_on = { target = FUL type = annex_everything } }
	}
	activate_mission = VIN_Dak_Doa_Objective
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_dak_doa_response_begin = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = NLF_Chanson_Ascendancy country_exists = VIN }
		VIN = {
			set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = 10 }
			vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		}
		log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|campaign=Dak_Doa|source=Chanson|supply_bonus=10"
	}
	hidden_effect = { vin_notice_dak_doa_launch = yes }
}

fre_dak_doa_response_record_result = {
	fre_dak_doa_response_clear_results = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NLF = { OR = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 3 } check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 5 } } } }
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Posture_Evacuate }
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Evacuated
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_GM100_Survived
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NLF = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 4 } } has_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Posture_GM100 }
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Relief_Successful
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_GM100_Survived
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NLF = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 4 } } }
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Held
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Posture_GM100 }
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Relief_Force_Mauled
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_GM100_Mauled
		add_manpower = -5000
		set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = -60 }
		fre_change_war_credits = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = -8 }
		fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Result_Post_Overrun
		set_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Isolated_Garrison_Lost
		add_manpower = -1000
		set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = -10 }
		fre_change_war_credits = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fre_patience_delta = -3 }
		fre_metropole_patience_change = yes
	}
}

	clr_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Response_Active

	fre_dak_doa_response_cleanup = yes
