}

nlf_dak_doa_record_result = {
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Clean
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Costly
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Aborted
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Failure
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 1 } } set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Clean }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 2 } } set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Costly }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 3 } } set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Aborted }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 4 } } set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Failure }
	else = { set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Result_Superseded }
}

nlf_dak_doa_apply_resolution_balance = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 10 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = -5 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = { add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 10 tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT } add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -5 tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 2 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = -2 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = { add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 5 tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT } add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -2 tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = 4 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = -5 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_pro_france = 5 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = { add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = -5 tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT } add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 5 tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT } }
	}
	FRA = { indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes }
}

nlf_dak_doa_finish = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Owns_FUL_War country_exists = FUL has_war_with = FUL }
		white_peace = FUL
	}
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Owns_FUL_War
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active
	remove_mission = VIN_Dak_Doa_Objective
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP }
		1286 = { add_victory_points = { province = 1328 value = -1 } }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Dak_Doa_Temporary_Objective_VP
	}
	set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 0 }
}

nlf_dak_doa_resolve = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Resolving
	set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Last_Outcome = nlf_dak_doa_outcome }
	nlf_dak_doa_record_result = yes
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRE } FRE = { fre_dak_doa_resolve_from_parent = yes } }
	nlf_dak_doa_apply_resolution_balance = yes
	hidden_effect = { vin_notice_dak_doa_result = yes }
	nlf_dak_doa_finish = yes
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Resolving
	country_event = { id = VIN_Dak_Doa.1 hours = 6 }
}

nlf_dak_doa_update_hold = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Posture_Evacuate } }
		add_to_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NLF_dak_doa_objective_held_trigger = yes }
		add_to_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 1 }
	}
	else = { set_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days = 0 } }
}

nlf_dak_doa_check_resolution = {
	if = {
		limit = { NLF_dak_doa_superseded_trigger = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 5 }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Dak_Doa_Posture_Evacuate } check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days > 6 } }
		set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 1 }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Hold_Days > 6 } NOT = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed > 45 } } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed > 21 } } } set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 1 } }
		else = { set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 2 } }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed > 45 } }
		set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 4 }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NLF_dak_doa_war_abandoned_trigger = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 3 }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
}

nlf_dak_doa_daily_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Resolving } }
		add_to_variable = { NLF_Dak_Doa_Days_Elapsed = 1 }
		nlf_dak_doa_update_hold = yes
		nlf_dak_doa_check_resolution = yes
	}
}

nlf_dak_doa_deadline_resolve = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Campaign_Dak_Doa_Active }
		set_temp_variable = { nlf_dak_doa_outcome = 4 }
		nlf_dak_doa_resolve = yes
	}
}

fre_lower_laos_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted }
		remove_ideas = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = LOS
			LOS = { has_country_flag = LOS_Bolovens_Template_Loaded }
		}
		LOS = { delete_units = { division_template = "Groupement Aeroporte Bolovens" } }
	}
	clr_country_flag = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reinforced
}

fre_lower_laos_reinforce_bolovens = {
	set_country_flag = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reinforced
	set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = -40 }
	fre_change_war_credits = yes
	# The airlifted groupement is raised under Royal Lao colours: the engine
	# will not spawn a CEFEO unit on ground controlled by a country CEFEO is not
	# at war with, and Lower Laos must not open a war or access deal with Laos.
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = LOS
			1187 = { is_controlled_by = LOS }
		}
		LOS = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = LOS_Bolovens_Template_Loaded } }
				set_country_flag = LOS_Bolovens_Template_Loaded
				division_template = {
					name = "Groupement Aeroporte Bolovens"
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
					}
					priority = 0
				}
			}
		}
		1187 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"Groupement Aeroporte Bolovens\" division_template = \"Groupement Aeroporte Bolovens\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
				owner = LOS
				prioritize_location = 1563
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { ic_bolovens_present = 0 }
		if = { limit = { LOS = { divisions_in_state = { state = 1187 size > 0 } } } set_temp_variable = { ic_bolovens_present = 1 } }
		log = "IC_AFK|LOWER_LAOS_RESPONSE|date=[GetDateText]|choice=reinforce|spawn=royal_lao|champasak_divisions_present=[?ic_bolovens_present|0]"
		hidden_effect = {
			LOS = { country_event = { id = IC_Notice_FRE.12 hours = 6 } }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Notice_FRE.12|to=LOS"
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = VIN }
				VIN = { country_event = { id = VIN_CEFEO.26 hours = 6 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_CEFEO.26|to=VIN"
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { fre_credit_amount = 40 }
		fre_change_war_credits = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|LOWER_LAOS_RESPONSE|date=[GetDateText]|choice=reinforce|spawn=skipped|reason=champasak_not_royal_lao|credits_refunded=40"
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension } }
			set_variable = { FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension = 3 }
	}
	log = "IC_AFK|LOWER_LAOS_RESPONSE|date=[GetDateText]|choice=reinforce|credits=[?FRE_War_Credits|0]"
}

vin_lower_laos_apply_atlante_pressure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Atlante_Hold_Applied }
			country_exists = FRE
			FRE = { has_country_flag = FRE_Atlante_Offensive_Active }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Atlante_Hold_Applied
		FRE = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension } }
				set_variable = { FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension = 0 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { FRE_Atlante_Hold_Extension = 4 }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted } }
				add_ideas = FRE_Lower_Laos_Reserves_Diverted
			}
		}
	}
}

vin_lower_laos_begin = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			vin_lower_laos_live_window_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Offensive_Launched }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Offensive_Launched
		set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Superseded
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_FRE_Garrison_Seen
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Atlante_Hold_Applied
		set_variable = { VIN_Lower_Laos_Days = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = -20 }
		vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRE }
			FRE = { country_event = { id = FRE_Lower_Laos.1 hours = 6 } }
		}
		vin_lower_laos_apply_atlante_pressure = yes
		hidden_effect = { vin_notice_lower_laos_launch = yes }
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Focus_Superseded
		set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Superseded
	}
}

vin_lower_laos_finish = {
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { fre_lower_laos_cleanup = yes }
	}
}

vin_lower_laos_resolve = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active }
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Superseded
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { vin_lower_laos_outcome = 1 } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Pressure
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = FRA }
				set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 18 }
				set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_amount = 15 }
				ic_afk_trace_score_communist = yes
				FRA = {
					add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 15 tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT }
					indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { vin_lower_laos_outcome = 2 } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Contained
			set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = 10 }
			vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Result_Superseded }
		hidden_effect = { vin_notice_lower_laos_result = yes }
		vin_lower_laos_finish = yes
	}
}

vin_lower_laos_daily_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_Diversion_Active }
		add_to_variable = { VIN_Lower_Laos_Days = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { vin_lower_laos_live_window_trigger = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { vin_lower_laos_outcome = 3 }
			vin_lower_laos_resolve = yes
		}
		else = {
			vin_lower_laos_apply_atlante_pressure = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_FRE_Garrison_Seen }
					country_exists = FRE
					FRE = { vin_lower_laos_fre_garrison_present_trigger = yes }
				}
				set_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_FRE_Garrison_Seen
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_Lower_Laos_Days < 45 } } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Lower_Laos_FRE_Garrison_Seen }
					set_temp_variable = { vin_lower_laos_outcome = 2 }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { vin_lower_laos_outcome = 1 } }
				vin_lower_laos_resolve = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

vin_laos_raid_modifier_sync = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VIN_Campaign_Overextension_Severe }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension_severe } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension_severe }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Active }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Last_Chance }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension_severe } } }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension_severe }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = laos_raid_overextension }
		}
	}
}

vin_all_in_luang_prabang_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					vin_all_in_corridor_overextended_trigger = yes
					vin_campaign_severe_overextension_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			vin_overextension_severe_apply = yes
		}
		else = {
			vin_overextension_severe_clear = yes
		}
	}
	vin_laos_raid_modifier_sync = yes
}

vin_all_in_luang_prabang_begin = {
	if = {
		limit = { vin_luang_prabang_launch_ready_trigger = yes }
		set_global_flag = Phase3
		set_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Chosen
		set_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active
		clr_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Resolved
		clr_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Success
		clr_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Failure
		clr_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Superseded
		set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor
		757 = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive } } }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = unplanned_offensive }
			}
		}
		ic_laos_raid_release_pathet_lao = yes
		LOS = {
			declare_war_on = {
				target = LAO
				type = annex_everything
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = VIN } }
				declare_war_on = {
					target = VIN
					type = puppet_wargoal_focus
				}
			}
		}
		ic_laos_raid_launch = yes
		set_variable = { global.Laos_Raid_Days_Elapsed = -30 }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = LAO }
			lao_ensure_templates = yes
			1295 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Kong Phan 5\" division_template = \"Kong Phan\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"
					owner = LAO
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Kong Phan 6\" division_template = \"Kong Phan\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"
					owner = LAO
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Kong Pathom 3\" division_template = \"Kong Pathom\" start_experience_factor = 0.35 start_equipment_factor = 0.9"
					owner = LAO
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				}
			}
			LAO = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 2000 producer = VIN }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 150 producer = VIN }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = -40 }
		vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|stage=commitment|supply=-40|deadline_bonus=30|extra_divisions=3"
	}
	else = {
		log = "IC_AFK|STAGE|date=[GetDateText]|owner=VIN|package=Luang_Prabang_All_In|stage=refused"
	}
}

vin_all_in_luang_prabang_resolve = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active }
		clr_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Active
		set_global_flag = Laos_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Resolved
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Last_Chance } }
			add_to_variable = { global.Laos_Raid_Last_Duration = 30 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VIN }
			VIN = {
				clr_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { vin_campaign_severe_overextension_trigger = yes } }
					vin_overextension_severe_clear = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { vin_luang_prabang_live_window_trigger = yes }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_global_flag = Laos_Raid_Attacker_Won
								has_global_flag = Laos_Total_War
							}
						}
						set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Success
						if = {
							limit = { country_exists = FRA }
							set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 27 }
							set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_amount = 40 }
							ic_afk_trace_score_communist = yes
							FRA = {
								add_to_variable = { StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 40 tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT }
								indochina_struggle_recalculate_total_anti_communist_score = yes
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Failure
						set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = -50 }
						vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
						if = {
							limit = { country_exists = FRE }
							FRE = { set_country_flag = FRE_Luang_Prabang_Callback }
							log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|package=Castor|source=Luang_Prabang|result=armed|discount=15"
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Superseded
				}
				hidden_effect = { vin_notice_luang_prabang_result = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

vin_all_in_luang_prabang_campaign_callback = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Success
			check_variable = { vin_camp_id = 11 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor_Credited_Lai_Chau }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor_Credited_Lai_Chau
		set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = 10 }
		vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|campaign=11|source=Luang_Prabang|supply=10"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Result_Success
			check_variable = { vin_camp_id = 3 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor_Credited_Dien_Bien }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_All_In_Luang_Prabang_Corridor_Credited_Dien_Bien
		set_temp_variable = { vin_supply_amount = 10 }
		vin_change_campaign_supply = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|CALLBACK|date=[GetDateText]|campaign=3|source=Luang_Prabang|supply=10"
	}
}

test_vin_all_in_luang_prabang_begin = {
	vin_all_in_luang_prabang_begin = yes
}

test_vin_all_in_luang_prabang_resolve = {
	vin_all_in_luang_prabang_resolve = yes
}

# Southern command contract. Call in NLF scope after setting nlf_command_choice:
# 1 Nguyen Binh, 2 Le Duan, 3 Le Duc Tho, 4 Tran Quoc Buu, 5 Kieu Cong Cung.
# This is the only writer of the five command flags.
nlf_set_southern_command = {
	clr_country_flag = Nguyen_Binh_Leader
	clr_country_flag = Le_Duan_Leader
	clr_country_flag = Le_Duc_Tho_Leader
	clr_country_flag = Tran_quoc_Buu_Leader
	clr_country_flag = Kieu_Cong_Cung_Leader
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 1 } }
		set_country_flag = Nguyen_Binh_Leader
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh }
			promote_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 2 } }
		set_country_flag = Le_Duan_Leader
		nlf_call_up_le_duan = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 3 } }
		set_country_flag = Le_Duc_Tho_Leader
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = NLF_Le_Duc_Tho }
			promote_character = NLF_Le_Duc_Tho
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 4 } }
		set_country_flag = Tran_quoc_Buu_Leader
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = NLF_Tran_Quoc_Buu }
			promote_character = NLF_Tran_Quoc_Buu
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 5 } }
		set_country_flag = Kieu_Cong_Cung_Leader
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = NLF_Kieu_Cong_Cung }
			promote_character = NLF_Kieu_Cong_Cung
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = NLF_Binh_Killed
			NOT = { has_country_flag = Nguyen_Binh_Leader }
			has_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh
		}
		retire_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = Le_Duan_Leader }
			has_character = VIN_Le_Duan
		}
		nlf_return_le_duan_north = yes
	}
	nlf_route_displaced_southern_cadres = yes
}

nlf_call_up_le_duan = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
			country_exists = VIN
			VIN = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
		}
		VIN = {
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = NLF
				character = VIN_Le_Duan
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = VIN_Le_Duan }
		VIN_Le_Duan = {
			set_portraits = {
				civilian = { large = "gfx/leaders/VIN/Le_Duan_50s.dds" }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Le_Duan_Cosvn_Role } }
			set_country_flag = NLF_Le_Duan_Cosvn_Role
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = VIN_Le_Duan
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc
					desc = "pol_desc_VIN_Le_Duan"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			promote_character = VIN_Le_Duan
		}
	}
}

nlf_return_le_duan_north = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIN }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = NLF_Le_Duan_Cosvn_Role }
			clr_country_flag = NLF_Le_Duan_Cosvn_Role
			VIN_Le_Duan = {
				remove_country_leader_role = {
					ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought_revsoc
				}
			}
		}
		VIN_Le_Duan = {
			set_portraits = {
				civilian = { large = GFX_Le_Duan }
			}
		}
		set_nationality = {
			target_country = VIN
			character = VIN_Le_Duan
		}
	}
}

# Whoever loses the command question leaves the southern movement.
nlf_route_displaced_southern_cadres = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NLF_orthodox_southern_command_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Southern_Purge_Done }
		}
		set_country_flag = NLF_Southern_Purge_Done
		news_event = { id = Binh_Cung_Leadership.7 days = 3 }
		nlf_displace_kieu_cong_cung = yes
		nlf_displace_tran_quoc_buu = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = Tran_quoc_Buu_Leader }
		nlf_displace_kieu_cong_cung = yes
	}
}

nlf_displace_kieu_cong_cung = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Cung_Displaced }
			has_character = NLF_Kieu_Cong_Cung
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		set_country_flag = NLF_Cung_Displaced
		VIE = { country_event = { id = Binh_Cung_Leadership.5 days = 90 } }
	}
}

nlf_displace_tran_quoc_buu = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = NLF_Buu_Displaced }
			has_character = NLF_Tran_Quoc_Buu
		}
		set_country_flag = NLF_Buu_Displaced
		set_global_flag = NLF_Buu_Defected
		country_event = { id = Binh_Cung_Leadership.8 days = 60 }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VIN }
			VIN = { country_event = { id = Binh_Cung_Leadership.8 days = 60 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = FRA }
			FRA = { country_event = { id = Binh_Cung_Leadership.9 days = 400 } }
		}
	}
}

# Binh's death costs the south its cohesion regardless of who succeeds him.
nlf_apply_binh_death = {
	set_country_flag = NLF_Binh_Killed
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = NLF_rev_cohesion }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = NLF_rev_cohesion
			add_idea = NLF_vietminh_disarray
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = NLF_nguyen_binh_command }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = NLF_nguyen_binh_command
			add_idea = NLF_post_binh_disorganization
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = NLF_guerilla_network }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = NLF_guerilla_network
			add_idea = NLF_fractured_command_structure
		}
	}
}

nlf_apply_binh_survival = {
	army_experience = 5
	if = {
		limit = { has_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh }
		NLF_Nguyen_Binh = {
			set_portraits = {
				army = { large = "gfx/leaders/NLF/1949/FIELD_MARSHALS/Nguyen_Binh_NLF.dds" }
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 1 }
	nlf_set_southern_command = yes
}

# The 1951 recall: Hanoi's verdict, the travel route, the ambush, and the 1953 handover.
vin_binh_course_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VIN_binh_course_live_trigger = yes
			date > 1951.4.15
			date < 1951.6.15
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Course_Opened }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Course_Opened
		country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.10
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			VIN_binh_course_live_trigger = yes
			date > 1951.7.20
			date < 1951.9.20
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Route_Set }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Recall_Asked }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Recall_Asked
		country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.11
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1951.9.28
			date < 1951.10.29
			NOT = { VIN_binh_outcome_recorded_trigger = yes }
		}
		vin_binh_resolve_recall = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1951.12.01
			country_exists = NLF
			NLF = { has_country_flag = NLF_Binh_Killed }
			NLF = { NOT = { NLF_southern_command_settled_trigger = yes } }
		}
		NLF = {
			set_temp_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 2 }
			nlf_set_southern_command = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			date > 1953.3.01
			date < 1953.4.01
			country_exists = NLF
			NLF = { has_country_flag = Le_Duan_Leader }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Cosvn_Handover }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Cosvn_Handover
		country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.20
	}
}

vin_binh_resolve_recall = {
	set_country_flag = Binh_Outcome
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		NLF = { set_country_flag = Binh_Outcome }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = NLF
			NOT = { check_variable = { VIN_Binh_Route = 3 } }
		}
		complete_national_focus = VIN_Nguyen_Binh_Ambush
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.4
					has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Censured
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.6
					any_country = { has_country_flag = FRE_Binh_Corridor_Interdiction }
				}
				vin_binh_recall_death = yes
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 14
					840 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = NLF
							is_controlled_by = VIN
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 4
					check_variable = { VIN_Binh_Route = 2 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2.5
					has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Escort_Committed
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					has_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Defended
				}
				vin_binh_recall_survival = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

vin_binh_recall_death = {
	NLF = { nlf_apply_binh_death = yes }
	country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.1
	NLF = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.1 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		VIE = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.2 }
	}
	complete_national_focus = VIN_Death_of_Nguyen_Binh
}

vin_binh_recall_survival = {
	NLF = { nlf_apply_binh_survival = yes }
	country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.3
	NLF = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.3 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VIE }
		VIE = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.4 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.4 }
	}
	complete_national_focus = VIN_Nguyen_Binh_Survives
}

# Binh is never recalled: no corridor, no ambush, and Hanoi's trust is spent instead.
vin_binh_keep_in_nam_bo = {
	set_variable = { VIN_Binh_Route = 3 }
	set_country_flag = VIN_Binh_Route_Set
	add_political_power = -75
	add_stability = -0.05
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		NLF = {
			add_war_support = 0.10
			add_manpower = 10000
			set_temp_variable = { nlf_command_choice = 1 }
			nlf_set_southern_command = yes
			country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.12
		}
	}
	country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.12
}

# Route mirror to Paris: the Deuxieme Bureau is offered the Stung Treng corridor.
vin_binh_offer_corridor_to_france = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRA
			OR = {
				FRA = { has_war_with = VIN }
				AND = {
					country_exists = NLF
					FRA = { has_war_with = NLF }
				}
			}
		}
		FRA = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.13 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FRE
			OR = {
				FRE = { has_war_with = VIN }
				AND = {
					country_exists = NLF
					FRE = { has_war_with = NLF }
				}
			}
		}
		FRE = { country_event = Binh_Cung_Leadership.13 }
	}
}

fre_binh_commit_corridor_interdiction = {
	set_country_flag = FRE_Binh_Corridor_Interdiction
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRE }
		FRE = { set_country_flag = FRE_Binh_Corridor_Interdiction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = FRA }
		FRA = { set_country_flag = FRE_Binh_Corridor_Interdiction }
	}
}

# The daily owner for Hanoi's post-Dien Bien Phu strategic fork.
vin_post_dbp_southern_war_check = {
	vin_post_dbp_legacy_compatibility = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VIN_Prepare_Dien
			has_global_flag = geneva_dbp_outcome_applied
			check_variable = { global.geneva_outcome_dbp = 2 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Strategy_Briefed }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Strategy_Briefed
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_War_Recognized
		country_event = { id = VIN_South.1 hours = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_War_Ended }
			NOT = { vin_post_dbp_southern_war_active_trigger = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_War_Ended
		if = {
			limit = { vin_post_dbp_southern_resistance_defeated_trigger = yes }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF_Defeated
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF_Ceasefire }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = NLF }
			NLF = { remove_ideas = NLF_Northern_Supply_Line }
		}
		response_package_validation_vin_south_log = yes
		country_event = { id = VIN_South.2 hours = 6 }
	}
	vin_post_dbp_escalation_cleanup_check = yes
}

# Old saves may contain a decision posture or an autonomous queue. Only an
# explicit old talks choice is allowed to inherit the focus-owned queue.
vin_post_dbp_legacy_compatibility = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Strategy_Compatibility_Checked } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Strategy_Compatibility_Checked
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_Response_Chosen
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Panic_Geneva_Suppressed
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued
						has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Pressed_For_Geneva
						has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Settlement_After_South
					}
				}
				clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Pressed_For_Geneva }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_Response_Chosen
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Negotiations_Chosen
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			set_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_Response_Chosen
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued }
				clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued
	}
}

# Focus-owned queue processor. Negotiations order the southern cadres to stand
# down: after 90 days a surviving NLF signs a ceasefire with VIE. A disarmed NLF
# is released at once. Both repairs wait until Hanoi itself is at peace.
vin_post_dbp_focus_geneva_queue_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Ordered }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Ordered
		set_country_flag = { flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Pending days = 90 value = 1 }
		log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=nlf_stand_down_ordered|delay_days=90"
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			has_war = no
			country_exists = VIE
			country_exists = NLF
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Regrouped }
			OR = {
				NLF = { num_divisions < 1 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Pending }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VIE = { NOT = { has_war_with = NLF } } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stood_Down
			log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=nlf_released|reason=already_at_peace"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NLF = { num_divisions < 1 } }
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Disarmed_NLF_Armistice
			log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=nlf_released|reason=disarmed"
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stood_Down
			log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=nlf_released|reason=stand_down"
		}
		vin_post_dbp_nlf_regroupment = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Ordered
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Stand_Down_Pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Gate_Block_Logged }
			NOT = { geneva_conference_vietnam_at_peace_trigger = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Gate_Block_Logged
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=gate_blocked|reason=VIN_at_war" }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE VIE = { has_war = yes } } log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=gate_blocked|reason=VIE_at_war" }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			geneva_conference_vietnam_at_peace_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded }
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Launched
		clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
		indochina_struggle_invite_countries_to_geneva = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
	}
}

vin_post_dbp_nlf_regroupment = {
	set_temp_variable = { regroup_divisions = 0 }
	if = { limit = { NLF = { num_divisions > 0 } } set_temp_variable = { regroup_divisions = 1 } }
	VIE = {
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = NLF } white_peace = NLF }
		annex_country = { target = NLF transfer_troops = no }
	}
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_NLF_Regrouped
	set_global_flag = geneva_nlf_outcome_applied
	add_manpower = 20000
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { regroup_divisions > 0 } }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"Southern Regroupment Regiment\" division_template = \"Trung doan Bo binh Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.4 start_equipment_factor = 0.7"
				owner = VIN
			}
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"Southern Regroupment Regiment\" division_template = \"Trung doan Bo binh Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.4 start_equipment_factor = 0.7"
				owner = VIN
			}
		}
	}
	country_event = { id = VIN_South.10 hours = 1 }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VIE } VIE = { country_event = { id = VIN_South.11 hours = 1 } } }
	set_temp_variable = { regroup_nlf_left = 0 }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } set_temp_variable = { regroup_nlf_left = 1 } }
	log = "IC_AFK|POST_DBP|date=[GetDateText]|stage=nlf_regrouped|nlf_had_divisions=[?regroup_divisions|0]|nlf_exists_after=[?regroup_nlf_left|0]"
}

vin_post_dbp_fund_southern_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
				has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued
				has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Pressed_For_Geneva
				has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Settlement_After_South
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
	}
	# A generic FRE/wrap-up pursuit can already be live without any legacy VIN
	# queue flag. The southern strategy always vetoes that pending conference.
	clr_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_Response_Chosen
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Funded_NLF
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Panic_Geneva_Suppressed
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
	clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Auto_Geneva_Queued
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit_Source = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = NLF }
		NLF = {
			add_ideas = NLF_Northern_Supply_Line
			add_manpower = 15000
			add_command_power = 25
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 4000 producer = VIN }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 500 producer = VIN }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 200 producer = VIN }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_writer = 14 }
		set_temp_variable = { ic_trace_communist = 25 }
		ic_afk_trace_score_pair = yes
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 25
				tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 50
				tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
			}
		}
	}
	response_package_validation_vin_south_log = yes
}

vin_post_dbp_press_for_geneva = {
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Southern_Response_Chosen
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Negotiations_Chosen
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Pressed_For_Geneva
	set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Focus_Geneva_Queued
	set_variable = { global.Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit_Source = 3 }
	set_global_flag = Indochina_Geneva_Pursuit
	set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 25 }
	geneva_add_communist_leverage = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over } }
		FRA = {
			add_to_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 150
				tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
			}
		}
	}
	response_package_validation_vin_south_log = yes
	vin_post_dbp_focus_geneva_queue_tick = yes
}

vin_post_dbp_escalation_cleanup_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Escalation_Chosen
			OR = {
				NOT = { country_exists = VIE }
				has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_announced
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_in_session
				has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Reunification_AI_Active
		if = { limit = { has_idea = VIN_National_Reunification_Preparation } remove_ideas = VIN_National_Reunification_Preparation }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = NLF } NLF = { remove_ideas = NLF_Northern_Supply_Line } }
		set_country_flag = VIN_Post_DBP_Preparation_Cleaned
	}
}

vin_patronage_window_open_trigger_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = geneva_conference_concluded }
			check_variable = { global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 9 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { vin_patronage_open = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { vin_patronage_open = 0 }
	}
}

vin_patronage_chinese_complete = {
	vin_patronage_window_open_trigger_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vin_patronage_open = 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese
		add_ideas = VIN_Chinese_Advisory_Group
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 1500 producer = PRC }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 producer = PRC }
		set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 25 }
		geneva_add_communist_leverage = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE|date=[GetDateText]|choice=chinese|campaign_supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = PRC }
				PRC = { country_event = { id = IC_Aid.1 days = 2 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Aid.1|to=PRC"
			}
			country_event = { id = VIN_Aid.1 days = 1 }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_Aid.1|to=VIN"
		}
	}
	else = {
		log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE_SKIPPED|date=[GetDateText]|choice=chinese|open=[?vin_patronage_open|0]"
	}
}

vin_patronage_soviet_complete = {
	vin_patronage_window_open_trigger_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vin_patronage_open = 1 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet
		add_ideas = VIN_Soviet_Military_Mission
		add_political_power = 100
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = VIN_Soviet_Doctrine_Bonus
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = land_doctrine
		}
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = VIN_Soviet_Artillery_Bonus
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = artillery
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 150 producer = SOV }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = auto_cannon_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { geneva_lev_amount = 40 }
		geneva_add_communist_leverage = yes
		log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE|date=[GetDateText]|choice=soviet|campaign_supply=[?VIN_Campaign_Supply|0]"
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = SOV }
				SOV = { country_event = { id = IC_Aid.2 days = 2 } }
				log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=IC_Aid.2|to=SOV"
			}
			country_event = { id = VIN_Aid.2 days = 1 }
			log = "IC_AFK|NOTICE|date=[GetDateText]|event=VIN_Aid.2|to=VIN"
		}
	}
	else = {
		log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE_SKIPPED|date=[GetDateText]|choice=soviet|open=[?vin_patronage_open|0]"
	}
}

vin_patronage_monthly_delivery = {
	vin_patronage_window_open_trigger_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { vin_patronage_open = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Chinese }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_1 amount = 400 producer = PRC }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 30 producer = PRC }
			log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE_DELIVERY|date=[GetDateText]|source=chinese|infantry=400|support=30"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VIN_Patronage_Soviet }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 25 producer = SOV }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = auto_cannon_equipment_1 amount = 8 producer = SOV }
			log = "IC_AFK|PATRONAGE_DELIVERY|date=[GetDateText]|source=soviet|artillery=25|anti_air=8"
		}
	}
}
